Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the complex framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 7. The resulting operator achieves bound O(592/n^2).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_7(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 7. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^592(S, F) is trivial. QED.
